TL;DR: 本文提出基于元谓词（meta-predicates）的认知类型系统，用于在临床决策规则的DSL中断言证据使用的合理性，通过AnFiSA平台在基因组变异解读中验证，实现了决策树到单峰级联的转换，在部署前捕获认知错误，并保持人类可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI医疗监管框架（如EU AI Act、FDA指南）要求临床决策支持系统不仅要准确，还要可审计。但现有的形式语言只验证句法和结构正确性，无法确保决策规则使用了认识论上恰当的证据。因此需要一种能在部署前约束证据使用类型的方法。

Method: 借鉴设计契约（design-by-contract）原则，引入元谓词（关于谓词的谓词）来断言DSL中临床决策规则的认识论约束。构建四维认识论类型系统（目的、知识领域、尺度、获取方法），元谓词规定每条规则允许的证据类型。在AnFiSA平台中实例化，将决策树重构为单峰级联（unate cascade），生成逐变量审计轨迹。

Result: 使用Brigham基因组医学协议对Genome in a Bottle基准中的560万个变异进行验证。决策树可重构为单峰级联，支持逐变量审计轨迹。元谓词验证在部署前即可捕获人为编写或AI生成的规则中的认识论错误。该方法与LIME/SHAP等事后解释方法互补：后者事后揭示使用了什么证据，前者事前约束可使用的证据类型。

Conclusion: 元谓词验证是迈向可审计决策逻辑的重要一步，不仅确保决策准确，还确保决策建立在恰当证据之上。虽在基因组学中验证，但该方法可推广至任何需要可审计决策逻辑的领域。

Abstract: \textbf{Background:} Regulatory frameworks for AI in healthcare, including the EU AI Act and FDA guidance on AI/ML-based medical devices, require clinical decision support to demonstrate not only accuracy but auditability. Existing formal languages for clinical logic validate syntactic and structural correctness but not whether decision rules use epistemologically appropriate evidence.
  \textbf{Methods:} Drawing on design-by-contract principles, we introduce meta-predicates -- predicates about predicates -- for asserting epistemological constraints on clinical decision rules expressed in a DSL. An epistemological type system classifies annotations along four dimensions: purpose, knowledge domain, scale, and method of acquisition. Meta-predicates assert which evidence types are permissible in any given rule. The framework is instantiated in AnFiSA, an open-source platform for genetic variant curation, and demonstrated using the Brigham Genomics Medicine protocol on 5.6 million variants from the Genome in a Bottle benchmark.
  \textbf{Results:} Decision trees used in variant interpretation can be reformulated as unate cascades, enabling per-variant audit trails that identify which rule classified each variant and why. Meta-predicate validation catches epistemological errors before deployment, whether rules are human-written or AI-generated. The approach complements post-hoc methods such as LIME and SHAP: where explanation reveals what evidence was used after the fact, meta-predicates constrain what evidence may be used before deployment, while preserving human readability.
  \textbf{Conclusions:} Meta-predicate validation is a step toward demonstrating not only that decisions are accurate but that they rest on appropriate evidence in ways that can be independently audited. While demonstrated in genomics, the approach generalises to any domain requiring auditable decision logic.

</details>


### [76] [Can MLLMs "Read" What is Missing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21277)
*Jindi Guo,Xi Fang,Chaozheng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMTR-Bench是一个评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）从视觉上下文重建被遮蔽文本能力的基准，包含2,771个跨语言、跨长度的测试样本，实验表明该任务对当前模型具有显著挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统问答任务无法独立评估MLLMs的视觉文本重建能力，因为其混杂了指令遵循能力。MMTR-Bench旨在通过消除显式提示，直接评估模型在布局理解、视觉定位和知识整合方面的内在能力。

Method: 设计无显式提示的文本重建任务，要求模型从单页或多页输入（如文档、网页）中恢复被遮蔽文本；构建包含2,771个跨语言、不同长度（单词/句子/段落）测试样本的基准；提出层级感知评估协议以适应多样性。

Result: 在对代表性MLLMs的实验评估中，MMTR-Bench呈现出显著挑战，尤其在句子级和段落级文本重建任务上表现尤为困难。

Conclusion: MMTR-Bench有效分离了文本重建能力与指令遵循能力，揭示了当前MLLMs在复杂视觉文本理解方面的局限性，为未来模型改进提供了评估方向。

Abstract: We introduce MMTR-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate the intrinsic ability of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to reconstruct masked text directly from visual context. Unlike conventional question-answering tasks, MMTR-Bench eliminates explicit prompts, requiring models to recover masked text from single- or multi-page inputs across real-world domains such as documents and webpages. This design isolates the reconstruction task from instruction-following abilities, enabling a direct assessment of a model's layout understanding, visual grounding, and knowledge integration. MMTR-Bench comprises 2,771 test samples spanning multiple languages and varying target lengths. To account for this diversity, we propose a level-aware evaluation protocol. Experiments on representative MLLMs show that the benchmark poses a significant challenge, especially for sentence- and paragraph-level reconstruction. The homepage is available at https://mmtr-bench-dataset.github.io/MMTR-Bench/.

</details>


### [77] [Spatial Metaphors for LLM Memory: A Critical Analysis of the MemPalace Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21284)
*Robin Dey,Panyanon Viradecha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemPalace是一个应用记忆宫殿空间隐喻的开源AI记忆系统，在LongMemEval基准上达到96.6% Recall@5，但分析发现其性能主要归因于逐字存储策略和默认嵌入模型而非空间隐喻本身。系统在逐字优先存储、低唤醒成本、零LLM写入路径和空间记忆隐喻应用方面做出真正创新，但存在夸大宣传的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将古代记忆宫殿（loci方法）的空间隐喻应用于大语言模型的长期记忆组织，旨在提供一种更直观、高效的记忆管理方式，解决LLM在长对话和知识持久化方面的不足。

Method: 采用四层记忆栈架构（Wings->Rooms->Closets->Drawers）形成宫殿层级；采用逐字优先存储哲学（verbatim-first），不依赖LLM推理进行写入；使用ChromaDB及其默认嵌入模型（all-MiniLM-L6-v2）进行向量检索；宫殿层级实质上是标准的向量数据库元数据过滤机制。

Result: 在LongMemEval基准上达到96.6% Recall@5，两周内在GitHub获得超过47,000星标；写入时无需任何LLM推理，唤醒成本仅为约170个token。但分析表明，其检索性能主要归因于逐字存储策略和ChromaDB默认嵌入模型，而非空间隐喻本身。

Conclusion: MemPalace在逐字优先存储哲学、低唤醒成本、零LLM写入路径和空间记忆隐喻应用方面做出了真正新颖的贡献，但其宣称的突破性性能存在夸大成分，反映了开源项目中营销速度超过科学严谨性的常见模式。竞争格局正如Mem0在2026年4月的进步所示，正在快速演变。

Abstract: MemPalace is an open-source AI memory system that applies the ancient method of loci (memory palace) spatial metaphor to organize long-term memory for large language models; launched in April 2026, it accumulated over 47,000 GitHub stars in its first two weeks and claims state-of-the-art retrieval performance on the LongMemEval benchmark (96.6% Recall@5) without requiring any LLM inference at write time. Through independent codebase analysis, benchmark replication, and comparison with competing systems, we find that MemPalace's headline retrieval performance is attributable primarily to its verbatim storage philosophy combined with ChromaDB's default embedding model (all-MiniLM-L6-v2), rather than to its spatial organizational metaphor per se -- the palace hierarchy (Wings->Rooms->Closets->Drawers) operates as standard vector database metadata filtering, an effective but well-established technique. However, MemPalace makes several genuinely novel contributions: (1) a contrarian verbatim-first storage philosophy that challenges extraction-based competitors, (2) an extremely low wake-up cost (approximately 170 tokens) through its four-layer memory stack, (3) a fully deterministic, zero-LLM write path enabling offline operation at zero API cost, and (4) the first systematic application of spatial memory metaphors as an organizing principle for AI memory systems. We also note that the competitive landscape is evolving rapidly, with Mem0's April 2026 token-efficient algorithm raising their LongMemEval score from approximately 49% to 93.4%, narrowing the gap between extraction-based and verbatim approaches. Our analysis concludes that MemPalace represents significant architectural insight wrapped in overstated claims -- a pattern common in rapidly adopted open-source projects where marketing velocity exceeds scientific rigor.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluating AI Meeting Summaries with a Reusable Cross-Domain Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21345)
*Philip Zhong,Don Wang,Jason Zhang,Kent Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个针对生成式AI应用的可复用评估流水线，实例化于会议摘要任务，包含五阶段分离架构。在114个会议、340个会议-模型对、680次评测的数据集上对多个GPT模型进行了基准测试，结果表明gpt-4.1-mini在准确率上最优（0.583），gpt-5.1在完整性和覆盖度上领先。与DeepEval对比基线相比发现基于参考的评分可能遗漏"无依据的具体细节"错误。部署跟踪显示gpt-5.4全面超越gpt-4.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI评估方法缺乏可复用的流水线，且大多依赖独立的claim评分器，缺乏对ground truth和评估器输出的结构化持久化管理，难以支持聚合分析、问题诊断和统计检验。

Method: 设计了五阶段可复用评估流水线：源数据获取、结构化参考构建、候选生成、结构化评分、报告输出。将ground truth和评估器输出都作为类型化、持久化的工件进行处理。在114个会议（含city_council、private_data、whitehouse_press_briefings三种类型）、340个会议-模型对、680次评测（使用gpt-4.1-mini、gpt-5-mini、gpt-5.1）上进行离线基准测试，并采用配对符号检验和Holm校正进行统计分析。

Result: gpt-4.1-mini取得最高平均准确率（0.583），gpt-5.1在完整性（0.886）和覆盖度（0.942）上领先。配对符号检验（Holm校正后）未发现准确率的显著优胜者，但确认gpt-5.1在保留指标上有显著提升。与DeepEval基线对比发现，基于参考的评分可能遗漏unsupported specifics错误。领域分析表明whitehouse_press_briefings是准确率挑战最大的领域。部署跟踪显示gpt-5.4在所有指标上超越gpt-4.1，且在保留指标上具备统计显著性。

Conclusion: 提出的评估流水线可复用且支持结构化分析和统计检验，能有效区分不同模型的差异化表现。基于参考的评分与基于claim的评分各有侧重，建议结合使用。在线反馈到评估的路径虽已文档化但未做定量评估，留待未来工作。

Abstract: We present a reusable evaluation pipeline for generative AI applications, instantiated for AI meeting summaries and released with a public artifact package derived from a Dataset Pipeline. The system separates reusable orchestration from task-specific semantics across five stages: source intake, structured reference construction, candidate generation, structured scoring, and reporting. Unlike standalone claim scorers, it treats both ground truth and evaluator outputs as typed, persisted artifacts, enabling aggregation, issue analysis, and statistical testing.
  We benchmark the offline loop on a typed dataset of 114 meetings spanning city_council, private_data, and whitehouse_press_briefings, producing 340 meeting-model pairs and 680 judge runs across gpt-4.1-mini, gpt-5-mini, and gpt-5.1. Under this protocol, gpt-4.1-mini achieves the highest mean accuracy (0.583), while gpt-5.1 leads in completeness (0.886) and coverage (0.942). Paired sign tests with Holm correction show no significant accuracy winner but confirm significant retention gains for gpt-5.1.
  A typed DeepEval contrastive baseline preserves retention ordering but reports higher holistic accuracy, suggesting that reference-based scoring may overlook unsupported-specifics errors captured by claim-grounded evaluation. Typed analysis identifies whitehouse_press_briefings as an accuracy-challenging domain with frequent unsupported specifics. A deployment follow-up shows gpt-5.4 outperforming gpt-4.1 across all metrics, with statistically robust gains on retention metrics under the same protocol. The system benchmarks the offline loop and documents, but does not quantitatively evaluate, the online feedback-to-evaluation path.

</details>


### [79] [Symbolic Grounding Reveals Representational Bottlenecks in Abstract Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21346)
*Mohit Vaishnav,Tanel Tammet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Bongard-LOGO基准测试比较了端到端视觉-语言模型（VLM）与基于符号输入的大语言模型（LLM）在抽象视觉推理上的表现，发现LLM在符号输入下准确率达90%以上，而VLM接近随机水平，表明表征瓶颈是抽象视觉推理的关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉-语言模型在抽象视觉推理任务（如Bongard问题）上失败的根本原因，究竟是推理能力不足还是表征能力受限。

Method: 提出Componential-Grammatical (C-G)范式，将Bongard-LOGO问题重新表述为基于LOGO风格动作程序或结构化描述的符号推理任务，将符号输入作为诊断探针而非实用多模态架构，系统比较VLM（像素输入）与LLM（符号输入）的表现，并进行输入格式、显式概念提示、最小视觉接地等消融实验。

Result: LLM在符号输入下准确率达90%以上（Free-form问题），而强视觉基线模型在匹配任务定义下接近随机水平。消融实验表明输入格式、显式概念提示和视觉接地等因素的影响远小于从像素到符号结构的转变。

Conclusion: 表征瓶颈是抽象视觉推理的关键障碍，而非推理能力不足；符号输入可作为可控的诊断性上界，为后续研究提供基准。

Abstract: Vision--language models (VLMs) often fail on abstract visual reasoning benchmarks such as Bongard problems, raising the question of whether the main bottleneck lies in reasoning or representation. We study this on Bongard-LOGO, a synthetic benchmark of abstract concept learning with ground-truth generative programs, by comparing end-to-end VLMs on raw images with large language models (LLMs) given symbolic inputs derived from those images. Using symbolic inputs as a diagnostic probe rather than a practical multimodal architecture, our \emph{Componential--Grammatical (C--G)} paradigm reformulates Bongard-LOGO as a symbolic reasoning task based on LOGO-style action programs or structured descriptions. LLMs achieve large and consistent gains, reaching mid--90s accuracy on Free-form problems, while a strong visual baseline remains near chance under matched task definitions. Ablations on input format, explicit concept prompts, and minimal visual grounding show that these factors matter much less than the shift from pixels to symbolic structure. These results identify representation as a key bottleneck in abstract visual reasoning and show how symbolic input can serve as a controlled diagnostic upper bound.

</details>


### [80] [ReaGeo: Reasoning-Enhanced End-to-End Geocoding with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21357)
*Jian Cui,Zhiyuan Ren,Desheng Weng,Yongqi Zhao,Gong Wenbin,Yu Lei,Zhenning Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReaGeo，一种基于大语言模型的端到端地理编码框架，通过将坐标转换为geohash序列将预测任务转化为文本生成问题，结合思维链机制和基于距离偏差奖励的强化学习，在显式地址查询和模糊相对位置查询上均表现优异，并具备非点几何区域的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段地理编码方法依赖文本或向量相似度检索地理数据库，存在工作流复杂、错误传播严重以及对结构化地理知识库高度依赖等问题，亟需一种端到端的解决方案。

Method: 将地理坐标转换为geohash序列，将坐标预测重构为文本生成任务；引入思维链（Chain-of-Thought）机制增强空间关系推理；采用基于距离偏差奖励的强化学习优化生成精度。

Result: 在单点预测中能准确处理显式地址查询；有效解决模糊的相对位置查询；对非点几何区域展现出强大的预测能力，验证了框架的通用性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: ReaGeo作为一种端到端地理编码框架，克服了传统多阶段方法的局限性，在多种地理编码任务上表现优异，具备良好的泛化能力和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes ReaGeo, an end-to-end geocoding framework based on large language models, designed to overcome the limitations of traditional multi-stage approaches that rely on text or vector similarity retrieval over geographic databases, including workflow complexity, error propagation, and heavy dependence on structured geographic knowledge bases. The method converts geographic coordinates into geohash sequences, reformulating the coordinate prediction task as a text generation problem, and introduces a Chain-of-Thought mechanism to enhance the model's reasoning over spatial relationships. Furthermore, reinforcement learning with a distance-deviation-based reward is applied to optimize the generation accuracy. Comprehensive experiments show that ReaGeo can accurately handle explicit address queries in single-point predictions and effectively resolve vague relative location queries. In addition, the model demonstrates strong predictive capability for non-point geometric regions, highlighting its versatility and generalization ability in geocoding tasks.

</details>


### [81] [Time, Causality, and Observability Failures in Distributed AI Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21361)
*Ankur Sharma,Deep Shah,David Lariviere,Hesham ElBakoury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 分布式AI推理流水线中，即使系统功能正确且性能良好，节点间微小时钟偏差（≥5ms）也会导致基于时间戳的可观测性出现因果错误，且偏差行为随时间动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式AI推理系统依赖时间戳进行可观测性分析，但时钟偏差对可观测性正确性的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在多节点AI推理流水线的单阶段注入受控时钟偏差，使用Kafka和ZeroMQ两种传输方式，观测不同偏差水平下的因果违反率及系统吞吐量、输出正确性等指标。

Result: 同步及≤3ms偏差时无因果违反，5ms偏差时出现明显违反；系统吞吐量和输出正确性几乎不受影响；长时间运行中负span率可能稳定或下降，表明有效偏差因时钟漂移而变化。

Conclusion: 可观测性正确性不仅依赖系统功能正确，更依赖精确的时间对齐；时间同步应作为分布式AI系统的一等关注点。

Abstract: Distributed AI inference pipelines rely heavily on timestamp-based observability to understand system behavior. This work demonstrates that even small clock skew between nodes can cause observability to become causally incorrect while the system itself remains functionally correct and performant. We present controlled experiments on a multi-node AI inference pipeline, where clock skew is introduced at a single stage. Results show that no violations are observed under synchronized conditions and up to 3 ms skew, while clear causality violations emerge by 5 ms. Despite this, system throughput and output correctness remain largely unaffected. We further observe that violation behavior is not strictly static. In longer runs, negative span rates may stabilize or decrease over time, indicating that effective skew evolves due to relative clock drift between nodes. Experiments were conducted using Kafka and ZeroMQ transports, with consistent results across both. Aeron is under active exploration but is not yet included in the completed validation set. These findings suggest that observability correctness depends not only on system functionality but also on precise time alignment, and that timing must be treated as a first-class concern in distributed AI systems.

</details>


### [82] [SemanticAgent: A Semantics-Aware Framework for Text-to-SQL Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21414)
*Qiang Gao,Zhenping Li,Anqi Zhuo,Yingxiao Zhao,Weibo Geng,Xiaosong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SemanticAgent框架，通过分析器、合成器、验证器三个模块实现语义感知的Text-to-SQL合成，将执行验证转变为可追踪推理过程，在语义质量评估上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL合成流程混淆了可执行性与语义有效性，语法检查和基于执行的验证可能保留执行成功但违反数据库语义的查询。

Method: 提出SemanticAgent框架，包含分析器、合成器、验证器三个专用模块，通过语义分析、逐步合成、诊断精炼三阶段协议，将执行验证转化为可追踪推理过程。

Result: 生成的合成数据在语义质量评估上持续优于先前合成方法，在语义要求高的基准测试上表现出更强的下游微调性能。

Conclusion: SemanticAgent通过语义感知的三阶段协议有效解决了可执行性与语义有效性的不一致问题。

Abstract: Existing text-to-SQL synthesis pipelines still conflate executability with semantic validity: syntactic checks and execution-based validation can retain queries that execute successfully while violating database semantics. To address these limitations, we propose SemanticAgent, a semantic-aware synthesis framework. SemanticAgent organizes synthesis around three specialized modules: an analyzer, a synthesizer, and a verifier. Through a three-stage protocol of semantic analysis, stepwise synthesis, and diagnostic refinement, SemanticAgent transforms execution-based validation alone into a traceable reasoning process. Our framework generates synthetic data that consistently outperforms prior synthesis methods under semantic-quality evaluation, leading to stronger downstream fine-tuning performance, especially on semantically demanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [83] [FairQE: Multi-Agent Framework for Mitigating Gender Bias in Translation Quality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21420)
*Jinhee Jang,Juhwan Choi,Dongjin Lee,Seunguk Yu,Youngbin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FairQE，一个基于多智能体的公平感知质量估计框架，通过动态偏误感知聚合机制，在保持原有QE模型优势的同时，有效缓解了性别模糊和性别明确场景下的系统性性别偏见，并在WMT 2023指标共享任务中取得了竞争性或更优的通用QE性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QE模型存在系统性性别偏见：在性别模糊上下文中偏好阳性表达，在性别明确时甚至给性别不一致的翻译更高分数。需要一种方法在不牺牲评估准确性的前提下缓解这些偏见。

Method: FairQE采用多智能体框架，包含三个关键组件：1) 性别线索检测；2) 生成性别翻转的翻译变体；3) 将传统QE评分与基于大语言模型的偏误缓解推理通过动态偏误感知聚合机制相结合。该设计以即插即用方式校准性别相关偏误。

Result: 在多个性别偏误评估设置上的实验表明，FairQE一致性地改善了性别公平性，超过了强QE基线。在WMT 2023指标共享任务的MQM基元评估中，FairQE取得了竞争性或更优的通用QE性能。

Conclusion: 性别偏误可以在不牺牲评估准确性的前提下得到有效缓解，从而实现更公平、更可靠的翻译评估。

Abstract: Quality Estimation (QE) aims to assess machine translation quality without reference translations, but recent studies have shown that existing QE models exhibit systematic gender bias. In particular, they tend to favor masculine realizations in gender-ambiguous contexts and may assign higher scores to gender-misaligned translations even when gender is explicitly specified. To address these issues, we propose FairQE, a multi-agent-based, fairness-aware QE framework that mitigates gender bias in both gender-ambiguous and gender-explicit scenarios. FairQE detects gender cues, generates gender-flipped translation variants, and combines conventional QE scores with LLM-based bias-mitigating reasoning through a dynamic bias-aware aggregation mechanism. This design preserves the strengths of existing QE models while calibrating their gender-related biases in a plug-and-play manner. Extensive experiments across multiple gender bias evaluation settings demonstrate that FairQE consistently improves gender fairness over strong QE baselines. Moreover, under MQM-based meta-evaluation following the WMT 2023 Metrics Shared Task, FairQE achieves competitive or improved general QE performance. These results show that gender bias in QE can be effectively mitigated without sacrificing evaluation accuracy, enabling fairer and more reliable translation evaluation.

</details>


### [84] [HiCrew: Hierarchical Reasoning for Long-Form Video Understanding via Question-Aware Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21444)
*Yuehan Zhu,Jingqi Zhao,Jiawen Zhao,Xudong Mao,Baoquan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiCrew提出了一种层次化多智能体框架，通过混合树结构保留时间拓扑、问题感知字幕机制生成语义描述以及规划层动态协调智能体协作，在长视频理解任务中取得显著性能提升，尤其在时序和因果推理方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临两大挑战：一是时空冗余和跨时间维度的复杂叙事依赖，现有结构化表示虽能压缩视觉信息但常牺牲时间连贯性，影响因果推理；二是现有多智能体框架依赖固定工作流，无法根据问题自适应调整推理策略。

Method: HiCrew框架包含三个核心模块：1）混合树结构（Hybrid Tree），利用镜头边界检测保留时间拓扑，同时在语义连贯片段内进行相关性引导的层次聚类；2）问题感知字幕机制（Question-Aware Captioning），合成意图驱动的视觉提示以生成精确语义描述；3）规划层（Planning Layer），根据问题复杂度动态选择智能体角色和执行路径，实现自适应协作编排。

Result: 在EgoSchema和NExT-QA数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性，在不同问题类型上均展现出强劲性能，尤其在时序和因果推理任务中取得了显著增益，这得益于层次化结构保留设计。

Conclusion: HiCrew通过层次化多智能体框架有效解决了长视频理解中时间连贯性丢失和推理策略僵化的问题，为复杂视频问答任务提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding remains fundamentally challenged by pervasive spatiotemporal redundancy and intricate narrative dependencies that span extended temporal horizons. While recent structured representations compress visual information effectively, they frequently sacrifice temporal coherence, which is critical for causal reasoning. Meanwhile, existing multi-agent frameworks operate through rigid, pre-defined workflows that fail to adapt their reasoning strategies to question-specific demands. In this paper, we introduce HiCrew, a hierarchical multi-agent framework that addresses these limitations through three core contributions. First, we propose a Hybrid Tree structure that leverages shot boundary detection to preserve temporal topology while performing relevance-guided hierarchical clustering within semantically coherent segments. Second, we develop a Question-Aware Captioning mechanism that synthesizes intent-driven visual prompts to generate precision-oriented semantic descriptions. Third, we integrate a Planning Layer that dynamically orchestrates agent collaboration by adaptively selecting roles and execution paths based on question complexity. Extensive experiments on EgoSchema and NExT-QA validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating strong performance across diverse question types with particularly pronounced gains in temporal and causal reasoning tasks that benefit from our hierarchical structure-preserving design.

</details>


### [85] [GeoMind: An Agentic Workflow for Lithology Classification with Reasoned Tool Invocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21501)
*Yitong Zhou,Mingyue Cheng,Jiahao Wang,Qingyang Mao,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoMind是一个工具增强型智能体框架，将岩性分类建模为顺序推理过程，通过感知、推理和分析三个模块协同工作，并采用细粒度过程监督策略优化中间推理步骤，在四个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有岩性分类方法采用静态、单步判别映射范式，缺乏地质先验知识，导致预测结果脱离地质现实，无法进行基于证据的诊断推理。

Method: 提出GeoMind框架，包含感知模块（将原始测井数据转化为语义趋势）、推理模块（从多源证据推断岩性假设）和分析模块（根据地层约束验证预测），由全局规划器根据输入特征自适应协调，并采用细粒度过程监督策略优化中间推理步骤。

Result: 在四个基准测井数据集上，GeoMind在分类性能上持续优于强基线方法，同时提供透明、可追溯的决策过程。

Conclusion: 将岩性分类建模为顺序推理过程并结合地质领域先验，能够有效提升预测的地质合理性和可解释性，过程监督策略对保证推理逻辑一致性至关重要。

Abstract: Lithology classification in well logs is a fundamental geoscience data mining task that aims to infer rock types from multi dimensional geophysical sequences. Despite recent progress, existing approaches typically formulate the problem as a static, single-step discriminative mapping. This static paradigm limits evidence-based diagnostic reasoning against geological standards, often yielding predictions that are detached from geological reality due to a lack of domain priors. In this work, we propose GeoMind, a tool-augmented agentic framework that models lithology classification as a sequential reasoning process. GeoMind organizes its toolkit into perception, reasoning, and analysis modules, which respectively translate raw logs into semantic trends, infer lithology hypotheses from multi-source evidence, and verify predictions against stratigraphic constraints. A global planner adaptively coordinates these modules based on input characteristics, enabling geologically plausible and evidence-grounded decisions. To guarantee the logical consistency of GeoMind, we introduce a fine-grained process supervision strategy. Unlike standard methods that focus solely on final outcomes, our approach optimizes intermediate reasoning steps, ensuring the validity of decision trajectories and alignment to geological constraints. Experiments on four benchmark well-log datasets demonstrate that GeoMind consistently outperforms strong baselines in classification performance while providing transparent and traceable decision-making processes.

</details>


### [86] [BioMiner: A Multi-modal System for Automated Mining of Protein-Ligand Bioactivity Data from Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21508)
*Jiaxian Yan,Jintao Zhu,Yuhang Yang,Qi Liu,Kai Zhang,Zaixi Zhang,Xukai Liu,Boyan Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Jinchuan Xiao,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioMiner是一个多模态生物活性提取框架，通过将语义理解与化学结构构建分离，结合化学结构引导的视觉语义推理范式，实现了文献中蛋白质-配体生物活性数据的高效自动化提取，并在多个应用场景中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中发布的蛋白质-配体生物活性数据对药物发现至关重要，但人工标注速度难以跟上文献快速增长。自动化提取面临两大挑战：需要跨文本、表格和图表解释生化语义，以及重建化学上精确的配体结构（如Markush结构）。

Method: 提出BioMiner多模态提取框架，将生物活性语义解释与配体结构构建明确分离。语义通过直接推理获取，化学结构通过化学结构引导的视觉语义推理范式解决——多模态大语言模型在化学基础视觉表示上操作以推断结构间关系，并将精确分子构建任务委托给专业化学工具。同时建立了包含16,457条生物活性记录的综合基准BioVista。

Result: BioMiner在BioVista基准上F1分数达0.32；三个应用验证：从11,683篇论文提取82,262条数据构建预训练数据库使下游模型提升3.9%；人机协同工作流使NLRP3高质量生物活性数据翻倍，28个QSAR模型平均提升38.6%并识别出16个新骨架候选化合物；在PoseBusters数据集上加速蛋白质-配体复合物活性标注，速度提升5.59倍，准确率提高5.75%。

Conclusion: BioMiner作为首个系统性的自动化生物活性数据提取框架，有效解决了文献中生物活性数据提取的关键瓶颈，在多个实际应用中展示了显著的价值，为药物发现数据基础设施建设提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Protein-ligand bioactivity data published in the literature are essential for drug discovery, yet manual curation struggles to keep pace with rapidly growing literature. Automated bioactivity extraction remains challenging because it requires not only interpreting biochemical semantics distributed across text, tables, and figures, but also reconstructing chemically exact ligand structures (e.g., Markush structures). To address this bottleneck, we introduce BioMiner, a multi-modal extraction framework that explicitly separates bioactivity semantic interpretation from ligand structure construction. Within BioMiner, bioactivity semantics are inferred through direct reasoning, while chemical structures are resolved via a chemical-structure-grounded visual semantic reasoning paradigm, in which multi-modal large language models operate on chemically grounded visual representations to infer inter-structure relationships, and exact molecular construction is delegated to domain chemistry tools. For rigorous evaluation and method development, we further establish BioVista, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 16,457 bioactivity entries curated from 500 publications. BioMiner validates its extraction ability and provides a quantitative baseline, achieving an F1 score of 0.32 for bioactivity triplets. BioMiner's practical utility is demonstrated via three applications: (1) extracting 82,262 data from 11,683 papers to build a pre-training database that improves downstream models performance by 3.9%; (2) enabling a human-in-the-loop workflow that doubles the number of high-quality NLRP3 bioactivity data, helping 38.6% improvement over 28 QSAR models and identification of 16 hit candidates with novel scaffolds; and (3) accelerating protein-ligand complex bioactivity annotation, achieving a 5.59-fold speed increase and 5.75% accuracy improvement over manual workflows in PoseBusters dataset.

</details>


### [87] [Unbiased Prevalence Estimation with Multicalibrated LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21549)
*Fridolin Linder,Thomas Leeper,Daniel Haimovich,Niek Tax,Lorenzo Perini,Milan Vojnovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明多校准（multicalibration）能够在协变量偏移下实现无偏的类别 prevalence 估计，解决了标准校准和量化方法在此场景下的偏差问题，并通过模拟和两项实证研究验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 prevalence 估计方法假设测量设备的误差率在不同人群中保持稳定，但这一假设在协变量偏移（covariate shift）下不成立，导致估计出现偏差。需要一种能在分布变化下仍能提供无偏估计的方法。

Method: 理论证明多校准（multicalibration）条件——即在输入特征上的条件校准——足以在协变量偏移下实现无偏 prevalence 估计。通过模拟实验和两项实证应用（美国社区调查的就业 prevalence 估计、四个国家的政治文本分类）进行验证。

Result: 模拟表明标准方法偏差随偏移幅度增大，而多校准估计器保持近乎零偏差。实证应用中，多校准显著减少了偏差，且校准数据需要覆盖目标人群的关键特征维度。

Conclusion: 多校准为协变量偏移下的 prevalence 估计提供了理论保证和实用工具，将公平性领域的理论工作与跨学科的经典测量问题联系起来，对使用分类模型（尤其是 LLM）进行 prevalence 估计具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Estimating the prevalence of a category in a population using imperfect measurement devices (diagnostic tests, classifiers, or large language models) is fundamental to science, public health, and online trust and safety. Standard approaches correct for known device error rates but assume these rates remain stable across populations. We show this assumption fails under covariate shift and that multicalibration, which enforces calibration conditional on the input features rather than just on average, is sufficient for unbiased prevalence estimation under such shift. Standard calibration and quantification methods fail to provide this guarantee. Our work connects recent theoretical work on fairness to a longstanding measurement problem spanning nearly all academic disciplines. A simulation confirms that standard methods exhibit bias growing with shift magnitude, while a multicalibrated estimator maintains near-zero bias. While we focus the discussion mostly on LLMs, our theoretical results apply to any classification model. Two empirical applications -- estimating employment prevalence across U.S. states using the American Community Survey, and classifying political texts across four countries using an LLM -- demonstrate that multicalibration substantially reduces bias in practice, while highlighting that calibration data should cover the key feature dimensions along which target populations may differ.

</details>


### [88] [Engaged AI Governance: Addressing the Last Mile Challenge Through Internal Expert Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21554)
*Simon Jarvers,Orestis Papakyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过 insider action research 在 AI 初创企业中探索 EU AI Act 在团队层面的落地实践，提出法律文本到行动的转化管道，揭示从业者对监管要求的三种感知模式（趋同、现有实践、脱节），并指出合规若不被视为系统质量和用户保护的有机部分则易沦为表面文章。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EU AI Act 已将 AI 治理要求写入法律，但从法律文本到软件开发团队的实际落地（"最后一英里"问题）缺乏实证研究；现有治理框架多停留在行业或组织层面，团队层面的实施证据极为稀缺。

Method: 采用 insider action research（内部行动研究）方法，嵌入一家 AI 初创企业；构建 legal-text-to-action pipeline：从法律文本提取要求 →  practitioners 参与评估与构思 → 集体评估确定实施优先级。

Result: 发现三种从业者感知模式：convergence（合规与发展优先级一致）、existing practice（当前工作已满足要求）、disconnection（要求被视为行政负担）；（2）从业者优先采纳服务用户或自身开发需求的要求，而将验证导向的要求视为打勾练习。

Conclusion: 监管要求存在被表面化处理的风险，除非从业者理解合规如何服务于系统质量和用户保护；专家协作可将治理从外部强加转变为共享所有权，并使先前不可见的治理工作变得可见和集体化。

Abstract: Under the EU AI Act, translating AI governance requirements into software development practice remains challenging. While AI governance frameworks exist at industry and organizational levels, empirical evidence of team-level implementation is scarce. We address this "Last Mile" Challenge through insider action research embedded within an AI startup. We present a legal-text-to-action pipeline that translates EU AI Act requirements into actionable strategies through internal expert collaboration by extracting requirements from legal text, engaging practitioners in assessment and ideation, and prioritizing implementation through collective evaluation. Our analysis reveals three patterns in how practitioners perceive regulatory requirements: convergence (compliance aligns with development priorities), existing practice (current work already satisfies requirements), and disconnection (requirements perceived as administrative overhead). Based on these patterns, we discuss when governance might be treated genuinely or performatively. Practitioners prioritize requirements that serve end-users or their own development needs, but view verification-oriented requirements as box-ticking exercises. This distinction suggests a translation challenge: regulatory requirements risk superficial treatment unless practitioners understand how compliance serves system quality and user protection. Expert collaboration offers a practical mechanism for transforming governance from external imposition to shared ownership and making previously invisible governance work visible and collective.

</details>


### [89] [To See the Unseen: on the Generalization Ability of Transformers in Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21632)
*Nevena Lazić,Liam Fowl,András György,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅解码器Transformer模型在抽象符号推理（命题逻辑推理）中遇到未见过变量名时性能下降的原因，发现未嵌入层（unembeddings）的表示坍缩是关键因素。基于此，作者提出结合小架构改动、数据多样性和（反）嵌入冻结/重置的技术组合，实现了对未见token的泛化，并在Gemma 3模型中验证了该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明仅解码器Transformer在命题逻辑推理任务中，当遇到训练期间未出现的变量名时泛化失败，已知原因之一是复制/生成未见token的困难。本文旨在揭示除token复制困难外，是否存在其他关键机制导致泛化失败。

Method: 通过理论分析和实证实验，证明未嵌入层（最后一层权重）中未见token的表示在训练过程中坍缩到几乎相同的向量，导致模型难以区分多个未见变量。基于此发现，设计组合方案：1）小架构改动以促进token复制；2）数据多样性；3）冻结或重置（反）嵌入层。在命题逻辑推理问题中进行受控实验验证。

Result: 1）证实了（反）嵌入坍缩是泛化失败的关键机制，并解释了"主动遗忘"（active forgetting）等启发式干预的有效性；2）提出的组合技术成功实现了对未见token的泛化；3）在Gemma 3系列模型中发现99个预留未使用token也存在类似的（反）嵌入坍缩现象，且这些token的嵌入相关性高，作为微调初始化效果差。

Conclusion: 未嵌入层的表示坍缩是Transformer模型在符号推理任务中泛化到未见token的重要障碍；所提出的组合方法可以缓解该问题；该现象不仅在合成实验中存在，也在真实开源模型（Gemma 3）中观察到，表明其具有实际应用意义。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of decoder-only transformer models to perform abstract symbolic reasoning; specifically solving propositional logic reasoning problems given in-context. Previous work demonstrated that models fail to generalize to problems involving variable names that were not observed during training, and it was shown that one reason behind this is the difficulty of copying (or generating) unseen tokens. We show both theoretically and empirically that a particular representational collapse also has a crucial role: the unembeddings (last-layer weights) of unseen tokens collapse to nearly the same vector during training. The collapse makes distinguishing multiple unseen variables difficult for the model (especially when the embedding and unembedding parameters are shared), and provides a mechanistic explanation for the effectiveness of existing heuristic interventions like "active forgetting", which periodically reset the token (un)embeddings. Based on these observations, we devise a combination of techniques, involving a small architecture change facilitating copying, data diversity, and freezing or resetting (un)embeddings, that achieves generalization to unseen tokens. We support our claims with extensive controlled experiments on propositional logic reasoning problems. Beyond synthetic experiments, we also observe evidence of (un)embedding collapse in the open-weight models in the Gemma 3 family, which includes 99 unused tokens reserved for downstream use. Empirically we find that the correlated embeddings of these tokens are a poor initialization for finetuning applications.

</details>


### [90] [GS-Quant: Granular Semantic and Generative Structural Quantization for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21649)
*Qizhuo Xie,Yunhui Liu,Yu Xing,Qianzi Hou,Xudong Jin,Tao Zheng,Tieke He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GS-Quant提出了一种用于知识图谱补全的框架，通过粒度语义增强和生成式结构重建模块，将知识图谱实体编码为层次化、语义连贯的离散代码，弥合连续图嵌入与大语言模型离散标记之间的模态差距，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在知识图谱补全任务中面临连续图嵌入与离散LLM标记之间的模态差距问题。虽然量化方法试图对齐这两种模态，但现有方法将量化视为扁平化的数值压缩，产生的编码在语义上纠缠不清，无法反映人类推理的层次化特性。

Method: GS-Quant包含两个核心模块：(1) 粒度语义增强模块，将层次化知识注入码本，使早期编码捕获全局语义类别，后期编码细化特定属性；(2) 生成式结构重建模块，在代码序列上施加因果依赖关系，将独立离散单元转化为结构化的语义描述符。通过将学习到的编码扩展至LLM词汇表，使模型能够以与自然语言生成同构的方式推理图结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，GS-Quant显著优于现有的基于文本和基于嵌入的基线方法。

Conclusion: GS-Quant通过生成语义连贯且结构分层的离散代码，有效解决了LLM在KGC任务中的模态对齐问题，验证了实体表示应遵循语言中从粗粒度到细粒度逻辑的洞见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown immense potential in Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC), yet bridging the modality gap between continuous graph embeddings and discrete LLM tokens remains a critical challenge. While recent quantization-based approaches attempt to align these modalities, they typically treat quantization as flat numerical compression, resulting in semantically entangled codes that fail to mirror the hierarchical nature of human reasoning. In this paper, we propose GS-Quant, a novel framework that generates semantically coherent and structurally stratified discrete codes for KG entities. Unlike prior methods, GS-Quant is grounded in the insight that entity representations should follow a linguistic coarse-to-fine logic. We introduce a Granular Semantic Enhancement module that injects hierarchical knowledge into the codebook, ensuring that earlier codes capture global semantic categories while later codes refine specific attributes. Furthermore, a Generative Structural Reconstruction module imposes causal dependencies on the code sequence, transforming independent discrete units into structured semantic descriptors. By expanding the LLM vocabulary with these learned codes, we enable the model to reason over graph structures isomorphically to natural language generation. Experimental results demonstrate that GS-Quant significantly outperforms existing text-based and embedding-based baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mikumifa/GS-Quant.

</details>


### [91] [Enabling and Inhibitory Pathways of University Students' Willingness to Disclose AI Use: A Cognition-Affect-Conation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21733)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动（CAC）框架，通过混合方法（546份问卷调查+22人访谈）探讨了大学生披露AI使用行为的心理机制。结果表明，心理安全感正向促进披露意愿，受公平感、教师支持和组织支持影响；而评价恐惧负向抑制披露意愿，受污名感知、不确定性和隐私担忧影响。制度清晰性和支持性教学实践促进开放，政策模糊性和负面评价恐惧则导致谨慎或策略性披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大学生在高等教育环境中披露AI辅助工作意愿背后的心理机制，揭示影响透明度的促进与抑制因素。

Method: 采用顺序解释性混合方法设计：量化阶段对546名学生进行问卷调查并使用结构方程模型分析，质性阶段对22名学生进行半结构化访谈以解释量化结果。

Result: 心理安全感显著提升披露意愿，受公平感、教师支持和组织支持正向影响；评价恐惧抑制披露意愿和心理安全感，受污名感知、不确定性和隐私担忧强化。质性分析补充了制度清晰性和支持性实践的作用。

Conclusion: AI使用披露受促进性和抑制性双重心理机制影响，支持性制度环境和明确指导对促进AI透明使用至关重要。

Abstract: The increasing integration of artificial intelligence (AI) in higher education has raised important questions regarding students' transparency in reporting AI-assisted work. This study investigates the psychological mechanisms underlying university students' willingness to disclose AI use by applying the Cognition--Affect--Conation (CAC) framework. A sequential explanatory mixed-methods design was employed. In the quantitative phase, survey data were collected from 546 university students and analysed using structural equation modelling to examine the relationships among cognitive perceptions, affective responses, and disclosure intention. In the qualitative phase, semi-structured interviews with 22 students were conducted to further interpret the quantitative findings. The results indicate that psychological safety significantly increases students' willingness to disclose AI use and is positively shaped by perceived fairness, perceived teacher support, and perceived organisational support. Conversely, evaluation apprehension reduces disclosure intention and psychological safety, and is strengthened by perceived stigma, perceived uncertainty, and privacy concern. Qualitative findings further reveal that institutional clarity and supportive instructional practices encourage openness, whereas policy ambiguity and fear of negative evaluation often lead students to adopt cautious or strategic disclosure practices. Overall, the study highlights the dual role of enabling and inhibitory psychological mechanisms in shaping AI-use disclosure and underscores the importance of supportive institutional environments and clear guidance for promoting responsible AI transparency in higher education.

</details>


### [92] [Thinking with Reasoning Skills: Fewer Tokens, More Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21764)
*Guangxiang Zhao,Qilong Shi,Xusen Xiao,Xiangzheng Zhang,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过总结和存储可复用的推理技能（reasoning skills）来替代从头推理（reasoning from scratch）的方法，在推理时检索相关技能引导模型，显著减少推理token数并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理LLM在解决新问题时常常花费大量token进行冗长的中间推理（如思维链），成本高且效率低。作者希望避免模型每次从零开始推理，转而利用已有经验来减少冗余探索。

Method: 1) 从大量推理过程中的深思熟虑和试错探索中提炼可复用的推理技能并存储；2) 在推理时检索与当前查询相关的技能；3) 用检索到的技能引导后续推理，避免走弯路。

Result: 在编码和数学推理任务上，该方法显著减少了推理token数量，同时提升了整体性能，降低了每次请求的成本。

Conclusion: 将推理经验总结为可复用的技能并在推理时检索，是一种比从头推理更高效且经济的范式，具备实际部署的潜力。

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs often spend substantial tokens on long intermediate reasoning traces (e.g., chain-of-thought) when solving new problems. We propose to summarize and store reusable reasoning skills distilled from extensive deliberation and trial-and-error exploration, and to retrieve these skills at inference time to guide future reasoning. Unlike the prevailing \emph{reasoning from scratch} paradigm, our approach first recalls relevant skills for each query, helping the model avoid redundant detours and focus on effective solution paths. We evaluate our method on coding and mathematical reasoning tasks, and find that it significantly reduces reasoning tokens while improving overall performance. The resulting lower per-request cost indicates strong practical and economic potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [93] [Who Defines "Best"? Towards Interactive, User-Defined Evaluation of LLM Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21769)
*Minji Jung,Minjae Lee,Yejin Kim,Sarang Choi,Minsuk Kahng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了LMArena基准测试数据集，发现其存在主题偏差、模型排名随提示类型变化以及偏好判断使用不当等问题，并设计了一个交互式可视化界面原型，让用户自定义评估优先级，从而改善LLM排行榜的透明度和上下文相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM排行榜由基准设计者设定评估优先级，未能反映不同用户和组织的多样化目标与约束，单一聚合分数掩盖了模型在不同提示类型下的行为差异。

Method: 对LMArena数据集进行深度分析，设计交互式可视化界面作为设计原型，并通过定性研究评估其效果。

Result: 数据集在主题上严重偏斜；模型排名在不同提示切片上存在差异；基于偏好的判断被使用时模糊了其预期范围。交互式界面能够提升透明度并支持更符合具体上下文的模型评估。

Conclusion: 交互式可视化方法能改善排行榜的透明度和上下文相关性，为设计和使用LLM排行榜提供了替代方案。

Abstract: LLM leaderboards are widely used to compare models and guide deployment decisions. However, leaderboard rankings are shaped by evaluation priorities set by benchmark designers, rather than by the diverse goals and constraints of actual users and organizations. A single aggregate score often obscures how models behave across different prompt types and compositions. In this work, we conduct an in-depth analysis of the dataset used in the LMArena (formerly Chatbot Arena) benchmark and investigate this evaluation challenge by designing an interactive visualization interface as a design probe. Our analysis reveals that the dataset is heavily skewed toward certain topics, that model rankings vary across prompt slices, and that preference-based judgments are used in ways that blur their intended scope. Building on this analysis, we introduce a visualization interface that allows users to define their own evaluation priorities by selecting and weighting prompt slices and to explore how rankings change accordingly. A qualitative study suggests that this interactive approach improves transparency and supports more context-specific model evaluation, pointing toward alternative ways to design and use LLM leaderboards.

</details>


### [94] [Inferring High-Level Events from Timestamped Data: Complexity and Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21793)
*Yvon K. Awuklu,Meghyn Bienvenu,Katsumi Inoue,Vianney Jouhet,Fleur Mougin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逻辑的新方法，用于从带时间戳的数据和背景知识中检测高级时间扩展事件。该方法使用逻辑规则捕获简单时间事件的存在和终止条件，并将其组合成元事件，通过约束识别不兼容事件组合并采用修复机制选择偏好一致集。原型系统使用回答集编程实现，在肺癌案例中验证了计算可行性和与医学专家意见的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从带时间戳的临床观测数据（如诊断和用药记录）中推断高级时间事件（如疾病发作和治疗过程）具有重要应用价值，但现有方法在处理事件的不确定性、时间扩展性以及事件间兼容性方面存在不足，亟需一种既能表达复杂时间关系又能处理不一致性的形式化方法。

Method: 采用逻辑规则定义简单时间事件的存在条件和终止条件，通过组合规则将简单事件构建为元事件；利用约束条件识别不兼容的事件组合，并设计修复机制以选择偏好一致的事件集；识别出可确保多项式时间数据复杂性的相关限制条件；使用回答集编程（ASP）实现原型系统。

Result: 在肺癌使用案例上的评估表明，该方法在计算可行性方面表现良好，且推理结果与医学专家意见高度一致。

Conclusion: 该框架虽受医疗领域需求驱动，但设计上具有通用性，可复用于其他领域。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a novel logic-based approach to detecting high-level temporally extended events from timestamped data and background knowledge. Our framework employs logical rules to capture existence and termination conditions for simple temporal events and to combine these into meta-events. In the medical domain, for example, disease episodes and therapies are inferred from timestamped clinical observations, such as diagnoses and drug administrations stored in patient records, and can be further combined into higher-level disease events. As some incorrect events might be inferred, we use constraints to identify incompatible combinations of events and propose a repair mechanism to select preferred consistent sets of events. While reasoning in the full framework is intractable, we identify relevant restrictions that ensure polynomial-time data complexity. Our prototype system implements core components of the approach using answer set programming. An evaluation on a lung cancer use case supports the interest of the approach, both in terms of computational feasibility and positive alignment of our results with medical expert opinions. While strongly motivated by the needs of the healthcare domain, our framework is purposely generic, enabling its reuse in other areas.

</details>


### [95] [Learning to Communicate: Toward End-to-End Optimization of Multi-Agent Language Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21794)
*Ye Yu,Heming Liu,Haibo Jin,Xiaopeng Yuan,Peng Kuang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DiffMAS框架，将多智能体系统中的潜在通信（键值缓存）作为可学习组件进行参数高效监督训练，在数学推理、科学问答、代码生成等任务上优于单智能体、文本多智能体和现有潜在通信方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统多将智能体间通信视为固定接口，而潜在通信（如键值缓存）虽有潜力但未与多智能体推理联合优化。

Method: DiffMAS：对多智能体潜在轨迹进行参数高效的监督训练，使智能体联合学习跨交互的信息编码与解释方式。

Result: 在AIME24上26.7%、GPQA-Diamond上20.2%，在数学推理、科学问答、代码生成和常识基准上一致提升推理准确率和解码稳定性。

Conclusion: 将潜在通信作为可学习组件能有效提升多智能体系统的推理性能与稳定性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built on large language models have shown strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, yet most work focuses on agent roles and orchestration while treating inter-agent communication as a fixed interface. Latent communication through internal representations such as key-value caches offers a promising alternative to text-based protocols, but existing approaches do not jointly optimize communication with multi-agent reasoning. Therefore we propose DiffMAS, a training framework that treats latent communication as a learnable component of multi-agent systems. DiffMAS performs parameter-efficient supervised training over multi-agent latent trajectories, enabling agents to jointly learn how information should be encoded and interpreted across interactions. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, scientific QA, code generation, and commonsense benchmarks show that DiffMAS consistently improves reasoning accuracy and decoding stability over single-agent inference, text-based multi-agent systems, and prior latent communication methods, achieving 26.7% on AIME24, 20.2% on GPQA-Diamond, and consistent gains across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [96] [Alignment has a Fantasia Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21827)
*Nathanael Jo,Zoe De Simone,Mitchell Gordon,Ashia Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文指出现代AI助手假设用户能清晰表达目标，但行为研究表明用户常在目标未完全形成时就与AI交互，导致"Fantasia交互"失败（即AI看似有用但实际上未能真正对齐用户需求）。论文提出AI应提供认知支持，主动帮助用户形成和完善意图，并提出了跨学科的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为研究长期表明，人们在与AI系统交互时往往目标尚未完全形成，但现有AI系统将用户的提示视为完整意图表达，导致表面有用但实际未对齐用户真实需求的问题（称为Fantasia交互）。

Method: 论文综合机器学习、界面设计和行为科学三个领域的见解，系统刻画Fantasia交互的机制与失败模式，分析现有干预措施的不足，并据此提出新的研究议程。

Result: 识别出Fantasia交互这一被忽视的对齐失败类型，论证了现有对齐研究将用户视为理性预言家的假设存在问题，并提出AI应转向提供认知支持以帮助用户形成和完善意图。

Conclusion: 对齐研究需要重新思考：不应将用户视为理性预言家，AI系统应通过认知支持主动帮助用户在时间进程中形成和完善目标意图。这需要机器学习、界面设计和行为科学的跨学科协作。

Abstract: Modern AI assistants are trained to follow instructions, implicitly assuming that users can clearly articulate their goals and the kind of assistance they need. Decades of behavioral research, however, show that people often engage with AI systems before their goals are fully formed. When AI systems treat prompts as complete expressions of intent, they can appear to be useful or convenient, but not necessarily aligned with the users' needs. We call these failures Fantasia interactions. We argue that Fantasia interactions demand a rethinking of alignment research: rather than treating users as rational oracles, AI should provide cognitive support by actively helping users form and refine their intent through time. This requires an interdisciplinary approach that bridges machine learning, interface design, and behavioral science. We synthesize insights from these fields to characterize the mechanisms and failures of Fantasia interactions. We then show why existing interventions are insufficient, and propose a research agenda for designing and evaluating AI systems that better help humans navigate uncertainty in their tasks.

</details>


### [97] [Bounding the Black Box: A Statistical Certification Framework for AI Risk Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21854)
*Natan Levy,Gadi Perl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI监管法规（如欧盟AI法案）虽要求高风险系统部署前证明安全性，但缺乏量化"可接受风险"的定义及验证方法。作者借鉴航空认证范式，提出两阶段框架：阶段一由主管机构设定可接受的故障概率δ和操作输入域ε；阶段二利用RoMA和gRoMA统计验证工具计算系统真实故障率的可审计上限，无需访问模型内部结构，适用于任意架构。该框架将AI风险监管转化为工程实践，与现有法律体系兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI监管法规（EU AI Act、NIST风险管理框架等）虽要求高风险系统安全性评估，但缺乏对"可接受风险"的量化定义和具体技术验证方法，导致开发者面临合规评估而无方法论可依的困境。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段一由主管机构形式化设定可接受的故障概率δ和操作输入域ε（具有民事责任含义的规范性行为）；阶段二利用RoMA和gRoMA统计验证工具，无需访问模型内部结构，计算系统真实故障率的可审计上界，适用于任意架构。

Result: 提出了将AI风险监管转化为工程实践的可操作性框架，该框架能与现有监管义务衔接，将责任上移至开发者，并与现行法律框架整合。

Conclusion: AI监管法规的架构已建立，但缺乏量化验证工具；本文提供的两阶段框架填补了这一空白，使高风险AI系统能够像航空领域一样进行可量化的安全认证。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence now decides who receives a loan, who is flagged for criminal investigation, and whether an autonomous vehicle brakes in time. Governments have responded: the EU AI Act, the NIST Risk Management Framework, and the Council of Europe Convention all demand that high-risk systems demonstrate safety before deployment. Yet beneath this regulatory consensus lies a critical vacuum: none specifies what ``acceptable risk'' means in quantitative terms, and none provides a technical method for verifying that a deployed system actually meets such a threshold. The regulatory architecture is in place; the verification instrument is not.
  This gap is not theoretical. As the EU AI Act moves into full enforcement, developers face mandatory conformity assessments without established methodologies for producing quantitative safety evidence - and the systems most in need of oversight are opaque statistical inference engines that resist white-box scrutiny.
  This paper provides the missing instrument. Drawing on the aviation certification paradigm, we propose a two-stage framework that transforms AI risk regulation into engineering practice. In Stage One, a competent authority formally fixes an acceptable failure probability $δ$ and an operational input domain $\varepsilon$ - a normative act with direct civil liability implications. In Stage Two, the RoMA and gRoMA statistical verification tools compute a definitive, auditable upper bound on the system's true failure rate, requiring no access to model internals and scaling to arbitrary architectures. We demonstrate how this certificate satisfies existing regulatory obligations, shifts accountability upstream to developers, and integrates with the legal frameworks that exist today.

</details>


### [98] [Nemobot Games: Crafting Strategic AI Gaming Agents for Interactive Learning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21896)
*Chee Wei Tan,Yuchen Wang,Shangxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型扩展香农游戏机器分类法的新范式，并介绍了Nemobot——一个交互式智能体工程环境，支持用户创建、定制和部署LLM驱动的游戏智能体，通过字典、严格求解、启发式和基于学习四类游戏展示了AI智能体的自我编程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏编程范式难以将现代LLM的能力系统化地融入游戏智能体设计，缺乏一个统一的框架来整合LLM在不同类型游戏中的策略生成与自我优化能力。

Method: 基于Claude Shannon的游戏机器分类法，构建Nemobot交互式工程环境，集成LLM驱动的聊天机器人；针对四类游戏分别采用状态-动作压缩、数学推理、经典minimax与众包数据结合、强化学习与人类反馈及自我批判等不同策略；支持工具增强生成和微调。

Result: Nemobot能够在字典游戏、严格可解游戏、启发式游戏和学习型游戏四类游戏中有效运行，展示了AI智能体通过众包学习和人类创造力迭代优化自身逻辑的能力，实现了某种形式的自我编程。

Conclusion: 该工作代表了向长期目标——自我编程AI——迈出的一步，证明了LLM驱动的智能体可以通过与环境、数据和人类的持续交互来迭代改进自身策略。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new paradigm for AI game programming, leveraging large language models (LLMs) to extend and operationalize Claude Shannon's taxonomy of game-playing machines. Central to this paradigm is Nemobot, an interactive agentic engineering environment that enables users to create, customize, and deploy LLM-powered game agents while actively engaging with AI-driven strategies. The LLM-based chatbot, integrated within Nemobot, demonstrates its capabilities across four distinct classes of games. For dictionary-based games, it compresses state-action mappings into efficient, generalized models for rapid adaptability. In rigorously solvable games, it employs mathematical reasoning to compute optimal strategies and generates human-readable explanations for its decisions. For heuristic-based games, it synthesizes strategies by combining insights from classical minimax algorithms (see, e.g., shannon1950chess) with crowd-sourced data. Finally, in learning-based games, it utilizes reinforcement learning with human feedback and self-critique to iteratively refine strategies through trial-and-error and imitation learning. Nemobot amplifies this framework by offering a programmable environment where users can experiment with tool-augmented generation and fine-tuning of strategic game agents. From strategic games to role-playing games, Nemobot demonstrates how AI agents can achieve a form of self-programming by integrating crowdsourced learning and human creativity to iteratively refine their own logic. This represents a step toward the long-term goal of self-programming AI.

</details>


### [99] [From Research Question to Scientific Workflow: Leveraging Agentic AI for Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21910)
*Bartosz Balis,Michal Orzechowski,Piotr Kica,Michal Dygas,Michal Kuszewski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种三层智能体架构，通过LLM将自然语言转化为结构化意图，再由确定性生成器产生可复现的工作流DAG，同时引入领域专家编写的"Skills"文档来约束词汇映射和参数优化。该架构将LLM的不确定性限制在意图提取阶段，在1000 Genomes和Hyperflow/Kubernetes上验证，意图准确率从44%提升至83%，数据传输减少92%，LLM开销低于15秒/0.001美元每查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流系统虽然实现了执行自动化（调度、容错、资源管理），但并未覆盖语义转换环节——科学家仍需手动将研究问题转化为工作流规范，这同时需要领域知识和基础设施专业能力。

Method: 提出三层智能体架构：(1) 语义层：LLM将自然语言解释为结构化意图；(2) 确定性层：验证过的生成器产生可复现的工作流DAG；(3) 知识层：领域专家编写"Skills"（Markdown文档），编码词汇映射、参数约束和优化策略。通过将LLM非确定性限制在意图提取阶段，确保相同意图始终产生相同工作流。

Result: 在150条查询的消融实验中：Skills使完全匹配的意图准确率从44%提升至83%；Skill驱动的延迟工作流生成将数据传输减少92%；端到端管线在Kubernetes上完成查询，LLM开销低于15秒，每次查询成本低于0.001美元。

Conclusion: 通过分离语义解释与确定性生成，并引入领域知识层（Skills），该架构有效弥合了自然语言研究问题与可执行工作流之间的鸿沟，在提升准确率的同时大幅降低成本和延迟，为科学工作流的语义自动化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scientific workflow systems automate execution -- scheduling, fault tolerance, resource management -- but not the semantic translation that precedes it. Scientists still manually convert research questions into workflow specifications, a task requiring both domain knowledge and infrastructure expertise. We propose an agentic architecture that closes this gap through three layers: an LLM interprets natural language into structured intents (semantic layer); validated generators produce reproducible workflow DAGs (deterministic layer); and domain experts author ``Skills'': markdown documents encoding vocabulary mappings, parameter constraints, and optimization strategies (knowledge layer). This decomposition confines LLM non-determinism to intent extraction: identical intents always yield identical workflows. We implement and evaluate the architecture on the 1000 Genomes population genetics workflow and Hyperflow WMS running on Kubernetes. In an ablation study on 150 queries, Skills raise full-match intent accuracy from 44% to 83%; skill-driven deferred workflow generation reduces data transfer by 92\%; and the end-to-end pipeline completes queries on Kubernetes with LLM overhead below 15 seconds and cost under $0.001 per query.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Sensitivity Uncertainty Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20903)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出敏感性-不确定性对齐(SUA)框架，通过衡量预测不稳定时的模型不确定性来识别大语言模型在对抗性和模糊输入下的失败，并引入SUA-TR训练方法和弃权规则以提升模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在对抗性和模糊输入下容易出现失败，而对抗敏感性与模糊性反映了一个共同问题：预测不稳定时模型不确定性未能相应提高，导致误校准。

Method: 定义SUA_theta(x)分数捕捉分布敏感性与预测熵之间的差异；通过最小化其正部来约束最坏情况下的扰动风险；提出SUA-TR训练方法，结合一致性正则化和熵对齐，并引入弃权规则。

Result: 在问答和分类等任务上，SUA比单独使用熵或自一致性更好地识别模型失败。

Conclusion: SUA框架是模型无关的，为提高不断演进的语言模型的可靠性提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose Sensitivity-Uncertainty Alignment (SUA), a framework for analyzing failures of large language models under adversarial and ambiguous inputs. We argue that adversarial sensitivity and ambiguity reflect a common issue: misalignment between prediction instability and model uncertainty. A reliable model should express higher uncertainty when its predictions are unstable; failure to do so leads to miscalibration.
  We define a scalar score, SUA_theta(x), capturing the difference between distributional sensitivity and predictive entropy. We show that minimizing its positive part bounds worst-case perturbed risk and relates to calibration error. We also formalize ambiguity collapse, where models produce overconfident outputs despite multiple valid interpretations.
  We introduce SUA-TR, a training method combining consistency regularization and entropy alignment, along with an abstention rule for safer inference. Across tasks including question answering and classification, SUA better identifies model failures than entropy or self-consistency alone.
  The framework is model-agnostic and provides a basis for improving reliability in evolving language models.

</details>


### [101] [Hidden Secrets in the arXiv: Discovering, Analyzing, and Preventing Unintentional Information Disclosure in Source Files of Scientific Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20927)
*Jan Pennekamp,Johannes Lohmöller,David Schütte,Joscha Loos,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了arXiv上270万份提交材料中因公开LaTeX源码而无意泄露的敏感信息（如不必要的文件、嵌入元数据、代码注释），发现几乎所有提交都存在此类问题，且现有清理工具效果不佳；作者提出了ALC-NG工具以全面移除不符合编译需求的文件、元数据和注释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv不仅公开预印本，还公开其LaTeX源码等文件，这种开放性可能导致敏感信息（如API密钥、Git历史、内部协调文档链接等）被无意泄露，但目前缺乏对此问题的系统性量化研究。

Method: 对所有270万份有源码的arXiv提交，从三个维度（不必要文件的包含、文件中嵌入的元数据、文件中无关内容如源码注释）系统分析信息泄露情况，并评估现有清理工具的效果。

Result: 几乎所有arXiv提交都存在某种形式的"隐藏"信息泄露，包括内部协调文档链接、API密钥、私人密钥、完整Git历史等；现有清理工具无法可靠地完成清理功能。

Conclusion: 作者提出了ALC-NG工具，能够全面移除编译LaTeX论文不需要的文件、元数据和注释，以缓解信息泄露问题。

Abstract: Preprints are essential for the timely and open dissemination of research. arXiv, the most widely used preprint service, takes the idea of open science one step further by not only publishing the actual preprints but also LaTeX sources and other files used to create them. As known from other contexts, such as GitHub repositories, and anecdotally exemplified for arXiv, making source code publicly available risks disclosing otherwise "hidden" information. Consequently, the public availability of paper sources raises the question of how much sensitive content is (unintentionally) disclosed through them.
  In this paper, we systematically answer this question for all 2.7M arXiv submissions with available source files across three dimensions of source file-induced information disclosure: (1) inclusion of unnecessary files, (2) metadata embedded in files, and (3) irrelevant content in files such as source code comments. Our analysis reveals that nearly every arXiv submission contains some form of "hidden" information. Notable findings range from links to editable web documents for internal coordination over API and private keys to complete Git histories.
  While different tools promise to remove such information from source files, we show that they fail to reliably achieve the intended cleaning functionality. To mitigate this situation, we provide ALC-NG to comprehensively remove files, metadata, and comments that are not needed to compile a LaTeX paper.

</details>


### [102] [SafeRedirect: Defeating Internal Safety Collapse via Task-Completion Redirection in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20930)
*Chao Pan,Yu Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeRedirect是一种系统级防御方法，通过重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它，有效对抗前沿LLM在合法专业任务中自发生成有害内容的"内部安全崩溃"（ISC）问题，将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM在执行需要有害内容的合法专业任务时，会出现内部安全崩溃（ISC）现象，即自发生成有害内容且安全失败率超过95%。现有输入级防御对此完全无效（100%失败率），标准系统提示防御也只能提供部分缓解，亟需更有效的防御方案。

Method: 提出SafeRedirect系统级防御方法，核心思想是重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它。具体包括：明确授予失败任务的权限、规定确定性硬停止输出、指示模型保留有害占位符未解析状态。在七种前沿LLM上针对三种AI/ML相关ISC任务类型的单轮对话场景进行评估。

Result: SafeRedirect将平均不安全生成率从71.2%降至8.0%，而最强可行基线为55.0%。多模型消融实验显示失败权限和条件特异性具有普遍重要性，其他组件的重要性因模型而异。跨攻击评估证实对ISC具有最先进防御性能，泛化能力至少与基线其他攻击族相当。

Conclusion: SafeRedirect通过重定向而非压制模型驱动力，在ISC防御上取得了显著效果，为LLM安全对齐提供了新的思路。代码已开源。

Abstract: Internal Safety Collapse (ISC) is a failure mode in which frontier LLMs, when executing legitimate professional tasks whose correct completion structurally requires harmful content, spontaneously generate that content with safety failure rates exceeding 95%. Existing input-level defenses achieve a 100% failure rate against ISC, and standard system prompt defenses provide only partial mitigation. We propose SafeRedirect, a system-level override that defeats ISC by redirecting the model's task-completion drive rather than suppressing it. SafeRedirect grants explicit permission to fail the task, prescribes a deterministic hard-stop output, and instructs the model to preserve harmful placeholders unresolved. Evaluated on seven frontier LLMs across three AI/ML-related ISC task types in the single-turn setting, SafeRedirect reduces average unsafe generation rates from 71.2% to 8.0%, compared to 55.0% for the strongest viable baseline. Multi-model ablation reveals that failure permission and condition specificity are universally critical, while the importance of other components varies across models. Cross-attack evaluation confirms state-of-the-art defense against ISC with generalization performance at least on par with the baseline on other attack families. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/SafeRedirect.

</details>


### [103] [Adaptive Defense Orchestration for RAG: A Sentinel-Strategist Architecture against Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20932)
*Pranav Pallerla,Wilson Naik Bhukya,Bharath Vemula,Charan Ramtej Kodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，通过自适应防御选择机制，在RAG系统中显著缓解安全性与检索效用之间的权衡，相比全时开启的防御堆栈，该方法能在消除成员推理攻击的同时将上下文召回率恢复至接近无防御基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在医疗、法律等敏感领域依赖私有领域知识，面临成员推理、数据投毒、内容泄漏等安全风险。简单启用所有防御会带来严重的效用损失（上下文召回率下降超过40%），因此需要一种能感知查询上下文的自适应防御机制来平衡安全性与检索效用。

Method: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，包含两个核心组件：Sentinel（哨兵）负责检测异常检索行为，Strategist（策略师）根据查询上下文选择性部署必要的防御措施，实现查询感知的自适应防御选择。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和五个编排模型上的实验表明：1) ADO消除了MBA风格的成员推理泄漏；2) 相比全静态防御堆栈，显著恢复了检索效用，接近未防御基线水平；3) 在数据投毒攻击下，最强ADO变体将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时将上下文召回率恢复至未防御基线的75%以上；4) 鲁棒性对模型选择敏感。

Conclusion: 查询感知的自适应防御策略能够显著降低RAG系统中的安全-效用权衡，为敏感领域部署RAG系统提供了更实用的安全方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare and law, where they rely on private, domain-specific knowledge. This capability introduces significant security risks, including membership inference, data poisoning, and unintended content leakage. A straightforward mitigation is to enable all relevant defenses simultaneously, but doing so incurs a substantial utility cost. In our experiments, an always-on defense stack reduces contextual recall by more than 40%, indicating that retrieval degradation is the primary failure mode. To mitigate this trade-off in RAG systems, we propose the Sentinel-Strategist architecture, a context-aware framework for risk analysis and defense selection. A Sentinel detects anomalous retrieval behavior, after which a Strategist selectively deploys only the defenses warranted by the query context. Evaluated across three benchmark datasets and five orchestration models, ADO is shown to eliminate MBA-style membership inference leakage while substantially recovering retrieval utility relative to a fully static defense stack, approaching undefended baseline levels. Under data poisoning, the strongest ADO variants reduce attack success to near zero while restoring contextual recall to more than 75% of the undefended baseline, although robustness remains sensitive to model choice. Overall, these findings show that adaptive, query-aware defense can substantially reduce the security-utility trade-off in RAG systems.

</details>


### [104] [Breaking Bad: Interpretability-Based Safety Audits of State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20945)
*Krishiv Agarwal,Ramneet Kaur,Colin Samplawski,Manoj Acharya,Anirban Roy,Daniel Elenius,Brian Matejek,Adam D. Cobb,Susmit Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的红队测试方法，通过对8个开源大模型进行激活导向搜索，发现Llama-3系列模型极易被越狱（最高91%），而GPT-oss-120B最为鲁棒，表明可解释性导向方法可用于系统性的安全审计，但也存在双重用途风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型安全审计多采用黑盒探测方式，难以系统性地发现模型内部机制中存在的安全漏洞，亟需基于模型内部表征的可解释性工具来深入评估模型鲁棒性。

Method: 采用Universal Steering (US)和Representation Engineering (RepE)两种可解释性方法，引入自适应两阶段网格搜索算法，在8个开源LLM（Llama-3.1-8B、Llama-3.3-70B-4bt、GPT-oss-20B/120B、Qwen3-0.6B/32B、Phi4-3.8B/14B）中寻找激活导向系数，基于有害查询集和LLM裁判协议评估越狱成功率。

Result: Llama-3.3-70B-4bt在US和RepE方法下分别有91%和83%的响应被成功越狱；GPT-oss-120B对两种可解释性攻击均表现鲁棒；Qwen和Phi系列中，小模型越狱率较低，而大模型更容易被攻击。

Conclusion: 可解释性导向方法可作为系统性安全审计的强大工具，但同时也揭示了其双重用途风险（既可用于审计也可用于攻击），表明大模型需要更强的内部防御机制。

Abstract: Effective safety auditing of large language models (LLMs) demands tools that go beyond black-box probing and systematically uncover vulnerabilities rooted in model internals. We present a comprehensive, interpretability-driven jailbreaking audit of eight SOTA open-source LLMs: Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, GPT-oss- 20B, GPT-oss-120B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-32B, Phi4-3.8B, and Phi4-14B. Leveraging interpretability-based approaches -- Universal Steering (US) and Representation Engineering (RepE) -- we introduce an adaptive two-stage grid search algorithm to identify optimal activation-steering coefficients for unsafe behavioral concepts. Our evaluation, conducted on a curated set of harmful queries and a standardized LLM-based judging protocol, reveals stark contrasts in model robustness. The Llama-3 models are highly vulnerable, with up to 91\% (US) and 83\% (RepE) jailbroken responses on Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, while GPT-oss-120B remains robust to attacks via both interpretability approaches. Qwen and Phi models show mixed results, with the smaller Qwen3-0.6B and Phi4-3.8B mostly exhibiting lower jailbreaking rates, while their larger counterparts are more susceptible. Our results establish interpretability-based steering as a powerful tool for systematic safety audits, but also highlight its dual-use risks and the need for better internal defenses in LLM deployment.

</details>


### [105] [Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994)
*Yannis Belkhiter,Giulio Zizzo,Sergio Maffeis,Seshu Tirupathi,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为函数劫持攻击（FHA）的新型攻击方法，通过操控智能体模型中的工具选择过程，强制调用攻击者指定的函数。该攻击对上下文语义不敏感，且可生成通用对抗函数，在5个模型上达到70%-100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI的快速发展，函数调用大语言模型（LLMs）被广泛用于调用外部函数。已有研究展示了提示词操控的漏洞，但智能体模型通过函数调用接口引入了更多安全风险，如数据篡改、无限循环和有害内容生成。本文旨在揭示函数调用过程中尚未被充分探索的安全漏洞。

Method: 提出函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操控智能体模型的工具选择过程，强制选择攻击者指定的函数。FHA对上下文语义不敏感，对函数集合具有鲁棒性，并可训练生成通用对抗函数，使其在多个查询和载荷配置下均能生效。

Result: 在5种不同模型（包括指令型和推理型变体）上进行实验，在BFCL数据集上实现了70%到100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。

Conclusion: FHA攻击揭示了智能体系统在函数调用接口上的严重安全漏洞，证明需要更强的安全护栏和安全模块来保护智能体系统。

Abstract: The growth of agentic AI has drawn significant attention to function calling Large Language Models (LLMs), which are designed to extend the capabilities of AI-powered system by invoking external functions. Injection and jailbreaking attacks have been extensively explored to showcase the vulnerabilities of LLMs to user prompt manipulation. The expanded capabilities of agentic models introduce further vulnerabilities via their function calling interface. Recent work in LLM security showed that function calling can be abused, leading to data tampering and theft, causing disruptive behavior such as endless loops, or causing LLMs to produce harmful content in the style of jailbreaking attacks. This paper introduces a novel function hijacking attack (FHA) that manipulates the tool selection process of agentic models to force the invocation of a specific, attacker-chosen function. While existing attacks focus on semantic preference of the model for function-calling tasks, we show that FHA is largely agnostic to the context semantics and robust to the function sets, making it applicable across diverse domains. We further demonstrate that FHA can be trained to produce universal adversarial functions, enabling a single attacked function to hijack tool selection across multiple queries and payload configurations. We conducted experiments on 5 different models, including instructed and reasoning variants, reaching 70% to 100% ASR over the established BFCL dataset. Our findings further demonstrate the need for strong guardrails and security modules for agentic systems.

</details>


### [106] [VRSafe: A Secure Virtual Keyboard to Mitigate Keystroke Inference in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21001)
*Yijun Yuan,Na Du,Adam J. Lee,Balaji Palanisamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VRSafe，一种能抵抗按键推断攻击的VR虚拟QWERTY键盘，通过引入假阳性按键干扰攻击者信息收集，并设计恶意登录检测器识别未经授权的登录尝试，在适度可用性开销下显著降低攻击准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VR环境中虚拟键盘的按键推断攻击研究较多，但缺乏有效且成本低廉的防御策略。

Method: 设计VRSafe键盘，在用户输入过程中故意引入假阳性按键干扰攻击者收集的数据；同时集成恶意登录检测器，高检测率、低时间与内存开销地识别利用按键推断攻击获取凭据的非法登录。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实用户研究，VRSafe能显著降低按键推断攻击的准确率，同时仅带来适度的可用性开销。

Conclusion: VRSafe是一种有效且成本高效的VR环境下抗按键推断攻击的防御方案，兼顾安全性与可用性。

Abstract: Password-based authentication is one of the most commonly used methods for verifying user identities, and its widespread usage continues in virtual reality (VR) applications. As a result, various forms of attacks on password-based authentication in traditional environments such as keystroke inference and shoulder surfing, are still effective in VR applications. While keystroke inference attacks on virtual keyboards have been studied extensively, few efforts have developed an effective and cost-efficient defense strategy to mitigate keystroke inferences in VR. To address this gap, this paper presents a novel QWERTY keyboard called \textit{VRSafe} that is resilient to keystroke inference attacks. The proposed keyboard carefully introduces false positive keystrokes into the information collected by attackers during the typing process, making the inference of the original password difficult. \textit{VRSafe} also incorporates a novel malicious login detector that can effectively identify unauthorized login attempts using credentials inferred from keystroke inference attacks with high detection rate and minimal time and memory cost. The proposed design is evaluated through both simulation experiments and a real-world user study, and the results show that \textit{VRSafe} can significantly reduce the accuracy of keystroke inference attacks while incurring a modest overhead from a usability standpoint.

</details>


### [107] [Layer 2 Blockchains Simplified: A Survey of Vector Commitment Schemes, ZKP Frameworks, Layer-2 Data Structures and Verkle Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21055)
*Ekleen Kaur,Marko Suvajdzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了统一的安全导向调查，系统性地将L2架构映射到其底层密码学安全，形式化分析了L2威胁模型（包括排序器活性、桥接漏洞和数据可用性失败），并深入剖析了ZK框架（Groth16、Plonk）及其承诺方案（KZG、IPA）的安全假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Layer-2协议在提升吞吐量的同时引入了由链下组件（排序器、桥、数据可用性机制）构成的复杂安全面，而现有文献[31][33]对此风险的认知是碎片化的，缺乏统一的安全视角。

Method: 采用统一的、以安全为中心的调查方法：将L2架构映射到其底层密码学安全，剖析从L1原语到现代L2核心的技术演进，形式化L2威胁模型，并分析ZK框架（Groth16、Plonk）及承诺方案（KZG、IPA）的安全假设（离散对数、计算性Diffie-Hellman、双线性Diffie-Hellman）。

Result: 形成了首个统一的安全导向L2调查，系统性地建立了L2架构与密码学安全之间的映射关系，完成了对关键密码学假设和技术栈的安全分析。

Conclusion: 本文作为一份可访问且严谨的参考，帮助研究者和开发者从深度密码学数学视角理解L2安全。

Abstract: Layer-2 (L2) protocols address the fundamental limitations of Layer-1 (L1) blockchains by offloading computation while anchoring trust to the parent chain. This architectural shift, while boosting throughput, introduces a new, complex security surface defined by off-chain components like sequencers, bridges, and data availability mechanisms. Prior literature[31][33] offers fragmented views of this risk. This paper presents the first unified, security-focused survey that rigorously maps L2 architecture to its underlying cryptographic security. We dissect the technical progression from L1 primitives to the core of modern L2s, analyzing the security assumptions(Discrete Logarithm, Computational Diffie-Hellman, Bilinear Diffie-Hellman) of ZK frameworks (Groth16, Plonk) and their corresponding commitment schemes (KZG, IPA). We formalize a comprehensive L2 threat model encompassing sequencer liveness, bridge exploits, and data-availability failures. This work serves as an accessible yet rigorous reference for researchers and developers to reason about L2 security from a deep crypto-mathematical perspective.

</details>


### [108] [Behavioral Consistency and Transparency Analysis on Large Language Model API Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21083)
*Guanjie Lin,Yinxin Wan,Shichao Pei,Ting Xu,Kuai Xu,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GateScope是一个轻量级黑盒测量框架，用于评估商业LLM API网关的行为一致性和运营透明度。通过对10个真实网关的测量，发现存在静默模型替换、内存退化、定价偏差和延迟不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方LLM API网关作为多厂商模型的统一接入点，其内部路由、缓存和计费策略不透明，用户无法验证请求是否由广告宣称的模型提供服务、响应是否忠实于上游API、账单是否准确。

Method: 设计GateScope黑盒测量框架，从四个关键维度审计网关：响应内容分析、多轮对话性能、计费准确性和延迟特征。

Result: 对10个真实商业LLM API网关的测量揭示了预期行为与实际行为之间的频繁差距，包括静默模型替换、降级的内存保留、偏离公开定价策略以及不同平台间延迟稳定性的显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前商业LLM API网关在行为一致性和运营透明度方面存在显著问题，GateScope为检测这些缺陷提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Third-party Large Language Model (LLM) API gateways are rapidly emerging as unified access points to models offered by multiple vendors. However, the internal routing, caching, and billing policies of these gateways are largely undisclosed, leaving users with limited visibility into whether requests are served by the advertised models, whether responses remain faithful to upstream APIs, or whether invoices accurately reflect public pricing policies. To address this gap, we introduce GateScope, a lightweight black-box measurement framework for evaluating behavioral consistency and operational transparency in commercial LLM gateways. GateScope is designed to detect key misbehaviors, including model downgrading or switching, silent truncation, billing inaccuracies, and instability in latency by auditing gateways along four critical dimensions: response content analysis, multi-turn conversation performance, billing accuracy, and latency characteristics. Our measurements across 10 real-world commercial LLM API gateways reveal frequent gaps between expected and actual behaviors, including silent model substitutions, degraded memory retention, deviations from announced pricing, and substantial variation in latency stability across platforms.

</details>


### [109] [Cross-Session Threats in AI Agents: Benchmark, Evaluation, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21131)
*Ari Azarafrooz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对AI智能体防护系统无法检测跨会话攻击的问题，提出了CSTM-Bench数据集、测量方法及Coreset Memory Reader算法与CSTM评估指标，实验表明仅有Coreset Memory Reader（K=50）能在跨会话场景下保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体防护系统对每条消息独立判断，导致攻击者可将单一攻击分散到数十个会话中绕过检测，因为只有聚合后才会显现有效载荷。亟需跨会话威胁检测能力。

Method: (1) 构建CSTM-Bench数据集：26种可执行攻击分类，按杀伤链阶段和跨会话操作分类，绑定7种身份锚点，含Benign混淆样本；(2) 测量方法：将跨会话检测形式化为信息瓶颈问题，对比会话级检测器与全日志关联器的性能；(3) 提出Coreset Memory Reader算法（K=50有界记忆）及CSTM指标（结合检测F1与CSR_prefix前缀稳定性）。

Result: 会话级检测器和全日志关联器在从稀释场景迁移到跨会话场景时召回率均下降约一半；仅有Coreset Memory Reader（K=50）能在两种场景下保持召回率；重新排序会破坏KV缓存前缀复用。

Conclusion: 跨会话攻击是现有防护体系的重要盲区，Coreset Memory Reader提供了可行解决方案；CSTM-Bench数据集和CSTM指标为后续研究奠定基础，需扩展到更多供应商和更大规模数据集。

Abstract: AI-agent guardrails are memoryless: each message is judged in isolation, so an adversary who spreads a single attack across dozens of sessions slips past every session-bound detector because only the aggregate carries the payload. We make three contributions to cross-session threat detection.
  (1) Dataset. CSTM-Bench is 26 executable attack taxonomies classified by kill-chain stage and cross-session operation (accumulate, compose, launder, inject_on_reader), each bound to one of seven identity anchors that ground-truth "violation" as a policy predicate, plus matched Benign-pristine and Benign-hard confounders. Released on Hugging Face as intrinsec-ai/cstm-bench with two 54-scenario splits: dilution (compositional) and cross_session (12 isolation-invisible scenarios produced by a closed-loop rewriter that softens surface phrasing while preserving cross-session artefacts).
  (2) Measurement. Framing cross-session detection as an information bottleneck to a downstream correlator LLM, we find that a session-bound judge and a Full-Log Correlator concatenating every prompt into one long-context call both lose roughly half their attack recall moving from dilution to cross_session, well inside any frontier context window. Scope: 54 scenarios per shard, one correlator family (Anthropic Claude), no prompt optimisation; we release it to motivate larger, multi-provider datasets.
  (3) Algorithm and metric. A bounded-memory Coreset Memory Reader retaining highest-signal fragments at $K=50$ is the only reader whose recall survives both shards. Because ranker reshuffles break KV-cache prefix reuse, we promote $\mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$ (ordered prefix stability, LLM-free) to a first-class metric and fuse it with detection into $\mathrm{CSTM} = 0.7 F_1(\mathrm{CSDA@action}, \mathrm{precision}) + 0.3 \mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$, benchmarking rankers on a single Pareto of recall versus serving stability.

</details>


### [110] [Position Paper: Denial-of-Service Against Multi-Round Transaction Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21169)
*Yuzhe Tang,Yibo Wang,Wanning Ding,Jiaqi Chen,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对以太坊打包服务的非对称拒绝服务攻击，通过利用交易间依赖关系和打包服务特性，开发出隐蔽、低成本、无风险的攻击方法，成功降低了构建者收益并减缓了区块生产速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝服务攻击设计因打包服务的多轮执行模型而无效，需要研究针对打包服务的新型攻击方法。

Method: 利用合约状态创建交易间依赖实现隐蔽性，滥用原子区块包含等打包服务特性降低资本和运营成本。

Result: 攻击达到高成功率，显著降低构建者收益，减缓区块生产速度。

Conclusion: 提出了针对已识别风险的缓解策略。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction-bundling services are a critical component of block builders, such as Flashbots Bundles, and are widely used by MEV searchers. Disrupting bundling services can degrade searcher experience and reduce builder revenue. Despite the extensive studies, the existing denial-of-service attack designs are ineffective against bundling services due to their unique multi-round execution model.
  This paper studies the open problem of asymmetric denial-of-service against bundling services. We develop evasive, risk-free, and low-cost DoS attacks on Flashbots' bundling service, the only open-source bundling service known to us. Our attacks exploit inter-transaction dependencies through contract state to achieve evasiveness, and abuse bundling-specific features, such as atomic block inclusion, to significantly reduce both capital and operational costs of the attack.
  Experimental results show that our attacks achieve high success rates, substantially reduce builders' revenue, and slow block production. We further propose mitigation strategies for the identified risks.

</details>


### [111] [Physically Unclonable Functions for Secure IoT Authentication and Hardware-Anchored AI Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21188)
*Maryam Taghi Zadeh,Mohsen Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了面向AI赋能的IoT和边缘计算系统的硬件根信任机制，比较了TPM、PUF、混合容器感知硬件信任根及纯软件安全方案，指出硬件信任方案在物理防篡改和设备防克隆方面优于软件方案，而PUF和混合信任锚为大规模AI-IoT系统提供了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
